### Sharing strategy now demonstrated

We share complete checked traces across cases whose used initial groups agree.
`RootBitsetTraceSupportV1` checks that BOTH owners and blockers of EVERY step lie
inside the support; its transport theorem preserves every used initial mask and
the final empty domain. `RootBitsetOriginalCaseSupportV1` applies this to the full
original Fin6/Fin34660 endpoint contract. Both modules are kernel/Std accepted.
No extra compatibility, bank, or terminal-empty assumption enters a case contract.

For cut coverage, `RootBitsetCoverageRectangleV1` checks common owner coverage
and the union of blocker columns once per rectangle. This replaces repeated
per-owner checks whenever the accepted facts have shared rectangular structure.
The first 49-case family needs **nine rectangles, five cut products, one trace**
instead of 4315 owner rows covering 5,195,082 pair uses. Every rectangle, cut,
and the complete five-step trace is already kernel/Std accepted.

This follows the checked finite-data/shared soundness architecture observed in
ofitzharding/sq17-lean, commit `d9653fd3c0a86633eac921793dfcae43379792b9`.
The adaptation is our support-sensitive trace transport and rectangular product
coverage; we do not claim that importing their proof establishes our geometry.
Historical source comparison and attribution are preserved below.

### Next shared program layer, pending actual pilot

`Sixpack/RootBitsetTraceV1.lean` implements a generic bitset pruning checker:
exact owner/blocker distinction, removal subset checks, proved rectangle masks,
XOR deletion, preservation of every compatible choice and an empty-terminal
contradiction. This replaces repeated per-case snapshot programs with one
proved algorithm plus checked data. Uniform contained compatibility must retain
legacy-to-contained forgetting; accepted contained banks cannot be upgraded to
legacy exclusions. An accepted terminal root bank can be the final ordinary
pruning step, eliminating a special refinement tail in each consumer.

The generic trace and real pilot are now accepted, as recorded above.
The shared original-case adapter is accepted; complete multi-step consumers remain required. Preserve all accepted programs and geometry units.

### Measured sharing strategy

A single executable registry containing1894 proof-carrying facts made even a
one-row check cost2138.8MiB, and32-row checks hit the3072MiB guard. A local60-fact
registry reduced the same one-row check to534.2MiB. Local registries alone are
not enough: a594-fact32-row pilot also hit the guard. The production packing
caps **64rows,128distinct facts,768references** per batch. Its largest pilots
used822.7MiB and its remaining batches786.3MiB. All1278rows fit36row modules.
Domain masks separately cap128domains and10000total root entries;672cut2masks
were checked in six modules. Never regroup accepted global mask ownership.

At product boundaries, keep both executable masks explicit in the `Exclusion`
constructor. `Sixpack.RootBitsetCoverageExplicitMergeV1` proves this interface
once. It prevents repeated unfolding through an accumulated chain of geometric
proof objects; the resulting full2.2million-pair merge checked at516.1MiB.
The old implicit-merge compilers were left untouched while a free fourth slot
checked this alternative. Their eventual results are not authoritative for the
explicit receipts. Keep all accepted rows and artifacts; only assembly changed.

This continues the checked-data/generic-soundness architectural lesson credited
below to ofitzharding's sq17-lean proof (pinned commit
`d9653fd3c0a86633eac921793dfcae43379792b9`). These mask and batching improvements
were measured here; no foreign geometric proof was copied or independently
claimed to have been built.

continue_root_derived_normal_source_bounds_v1.py,super64086. Clamp arbitrary
